# Shopify server error coverage — ARCH-057-SHOPIFY-011

Final source reconciliation, 2026-11-05. Scope is the application source at this task branch, not a production observability or remote retention certification. All 200 `app` JS/TS/JSX/TSX modules and the read-only `observability.mjs` preload were scanned using the installed TypeScript parser; the rows include catch/rejection handlers, async functions, constructors, listeners, provider calls/status branches, explicit throws, typed fallbacks, and module propagation where there is no such node. Multiple rows can describe one failure as it moves to its owner. The source anchors below identify the exact symbol and final line; snippets identify the branch, not logged payloads.

## Evidence and field contract

**E** means safe original Error name/message, bounded causeChain (up to four), available real code (the existing central databaseCode field can contain driver codes), operation/stage/origin, safe tenant/queue identity only when available, and honest redactionApplied/truncated. Provider adapters also retain actual HTTP status/request ID/provider code where supplied. Never fabricate upstream text, codes, causes, or request IDs. Expected input/business branches need no error log. A row naming multiple semantic owners follows the actual operation to its source owner; the final framework owner covers unexpected propagation, and existing original-error marking avoids repeating full evidence.

- **B**: `tests/unit/server-error-boundaries.test.ts`: loader/action source/code/cause, URL/header omission, original shell rejection, render500/body, browser/bot201, unchanged abort timer, redirect/4xx suppression, source ownership deduplication, throwing sink.
- **F**: `tests/unit/server-error-fallbacks.test.ts`: Redis/PG connect/query/read/cleanup, constructor rejection, own timeout, pending read/emitter/cleanup, notification persistence/dispatch/invariant, postcommit reconciliation, taxonomy transport/GraphQL/parser/mapping, onboarding503/rethrow, probe success/network/non-success and response privacy; normal and throwing sinks.
- **P**: `tests/unit/server-error-privacy.test.ts`: installed SDK callback all supplied severities, safe source marker, opaque context/prefix omission, bootstrap fail closed, actual DB wrapper/cause/code, keyring/decrypted JSON SyntaxError echo omission, optional central projection ownership compatibility.
- **B/F/P plus existing configuration/bootstrap/pending/health suites**: `npm run test:server-diagnostics` — **116 passed, 7 files**.
- **R**: `npm test -- tests/unit/merchant-support-service.test.ts tests/unit/merchant-support-observability.test.ts tests/unit/merchant-knowledge-queue.test.ts tests/unit/merchant-knowledge-observability.test.ts tests/unit/services/billing/provider-diagnostics.test.ts tests/unit/services/billing-reconciliation.service.test.ts tests/unit/services/billing/subscription-ended-notification.service.test.ts tests/unit/shopify-taxonomy-suggestion.test.ts tests/unit/routes/onboarding-resume.test.ts` — **73 passed, 9 files**.

“Inherited … inspected” means source/test ownership was reconciled against accepted predecessor behavior, not that every row was independently fault-injected in this task. Common B evidence verifies the final framework owner for uncaught route failures. Local synthetic ports exercise failures without contacting Shopify, PostgreSQL, Redis, R2 or a remote collector. No new SQL/query-count or integration coverage is claimed.

## Outcomes and explicit privacy limitations

- Framework Responses 300–499 remain expected control flow. SSR still rejects the original shell error, sets500 on render failure, and aborts at6000ms. No request URL/query/header/body is logged.
- Configuration wrappers retain the original cause and public code/key. Parser echoes are omitted in the diagnostic projection while the actual thrown SyntaxError remains the cause. No plaintext, seal/keyring material or ciphertext is logged.
- Health/pending checks retain existing deadlines and Boolean/unavailable response policies. Attempt errors own the diagnostic; matching emitter errors are not repeated. Cleanup errors cannot replace the primary result. Producer queues now have source-owned idle error listeners. No process-level keep-running handler was installed.
- Notification dispatch and postcommit scheduling remain best effort. Failed notice delivery is not claimed to retry automatically.
- The installed Shopify13.1.0 callback is `(severity, msg)`; context has already been flattened. There is no structured-context argument. The adapter omits the opaque suffix beginning ` | {`, and opaque body/session/request/JSON prefix content, with explicit redaction metadata/reasonCode. It retains independently safe supplied text and severity through the existing projection. This authorized privacy omission is a concrete SDK capability limitation, not reconstructed context or an invented provider message. Actual thrown SDK errors retain their original propagation and named owner.
- The probe still fetches its actual configured target once and returns HTTP200/no-store with the same keys/types/statuses. Its displayed/logged target is redacted; actual network cause/code is owned internally, and non-success logs only actual status plus the fixed probe stage.
- `observability.mjs` delegates instrumentation to pinned Shared initNodeObservability. Exporter/sink isolation remains Shared-owned; this task neither overrides runtime fatal behavior nor proves exporter delivery.

## Final inventory

1147 source sites. Classification counts below include duplicate propagation stages and pure/browser modules; they are not distinct incidents.

- expected business/input control flow: 206
- propagated to named logged owner: 779
- operational failure logged: 150
- logger/telemetry-internal isolation: 12
